490: async function ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify=true}={}){
491:   uepDiagRecord('session.ensure.start',{verify,online:navigator.onLine});
492:   if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
493:     const missing={authenticated:false,reason:'School Read API 세션 기능 없음'};
494:     uepDiagRecord('session.bridge.missing',missing);return missing;
495:   }
496:   let status=null;
497:   try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify});uepDiagRecord('session.status.before',status);}
498:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.before.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
499:   if(status?.authenticated)return status;
500:   const saved=state?.auth?.user||state?.auth?.rememberedUser||state?.settings?.userProfile||{};
501:   const name=String(saved?.name||state?.settings?.loginUserName||state?.settings?.teacherName||'').trim();
502:   const email=String(saved?.email||'').trim();
503:   uepDiagRecord('session.identity.available',{hasName:Boolean(name),hasEmail:Boolean(email),source:state?.auth?.user?'auth.user':state?.auth?.rememberedUser?'auth.rememberedUser':'settings.userProfile'});

505:     const missing={authenticated:false,reason:!name||!email?'저장된 담임 이름·이메일 없음':'School Read 로그인 기능 없음'};
506:     uepDiagRecord('session.login.skipped',missing);return status||missing;

510:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.login.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 예외'};}
511:   if(!login?.ok)return {authenticated:false,reason:login?.message||login?.reason||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 실패'};
512:   try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('session.status.after',status);}
513:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.after.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'로그인 후 세션 검증 예외'};}
514:   return status||{authenticated:false};
515: }

518:     ? withStartupTimeout(ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}), 8000, null)
519:         .then((status) => {
520:           if(status) googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status.endpointReady!==false};

522:         })

581: function applyProgramWritePatch08172(target,patch){
582:   if(!target||!patch)return target;
583:   for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(patch)){if(value!==undefined)target[key]=value;}
584:   if(patch.commonReportFormUrl!==undefined)target.reportFormUrl=patch.commonReportFormUrl;
585:   if(patch.activityType!==undefined)target.type=patch.activityType;
586:   if(patch.nightLinked!==undefined)target.affectsAttendance=Boolean(patch.nightLinked);
587:   return target;
588: }
